[Event 508: Weapon Power - Max]
;     This event is the cause of the soft-locking that happens if you pull tricks to get more orbs then your supposed to.
;     Having this event called ends up terminating the event chain early and you won't be removed from boss arenas.
;
;     It is supposed to be an error handling event. The Japanese version of this event includes text that says "Weapon Power - Max!"
;     It still returns incorrectly and terminates the event chain though. Probably why they didn't bother translating the text.
;     Adding OpCode 02 to return to the previous event would allow this to work properly.
0A598A: 50                             ;Open Text Window.
0A598B: 00                             ;End Of Event.

[Event 509: Got Weapon Orb Ending]
0A598C: C29380A99282C8
         ' s _ O r b !

0A5993: 40 02D8 00 88                  ;Play Sound Effect: 02D8 (Orb Obtained Chime), Fade: 00, Volume: 88.
0A5998: 28 08                          ;Halt Event Processing For 08 Ticks.
0A599A: 51                             ;Close Text Window.
0A599B: 02                             ;Return To Calling Event.
0A599C: 00                             ;End Of Event.

[Event: Localization Leftover]
;     Chopped off part of one of the error messages above.
CA/B574: 858088859285C800
          e _ h e r e !##

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
;     Everything from here to 0A/BDFF is leftover localization garbage. The JP version contained all boss frames within this bank.
;     Since English required more space many boss frames were moved to other banks (Primarily Bank 07).
;     Starting at 0A/BE00 is a pointer table for boss palette scripts and after that another list of boss frame structures.
;     These are all used in this version of the game.
